07001001  janamejaya uvAca
07001001a tam apratimasattvaujobalavIryaparAkramam
07001001c hataM devavrataM zrutvA pAJcAlyena zikhaNDinA
07001002a dhRtarASTras tadA rAjA zokavyAkulacetanaH
07001002c kim aceSTata viprarSe hate pitari vIryavAn
07001003a tasya putro hi bhagavan bhISmadroNamukhai rathaiH
07001003c parAjitya maheSvAsAn pANDavAn rAjyam icchati
07001004a tasmin hate tu bhagavan ketau sarvadhanuSmatAm
07001004c yad aceSTata kauravyas tan me brUhi dvijottama
07001005  vaizaMpAyana uvAca
07001005a nihataM pitaraM zrutvA dhRtarASTro janAdhipaH
07001005c lebhe na zAntiM kauravyaz cintAzokaparAyaNaH
07001006a tasya cintayato duHkham anizaM pArthivasya tat
07001006c AjagAma vizuddhAtmA punar gAvalgaNis tadA
07001007a zibirAt saMjayaM prAptaM nizi nAgAhvayaM puram
07001007c Ambikeyo mahArAja dhRtarASTro 'nvapRcchata

07001008c putrANAM jayam AkAGkSan vilalApAturo yathA
07001009  dhRtarASTra uvAca
07001009a saMsAdhya tu mahAtmAnaM bhISmaM bhImaparAkramam
07001009c kim akArSuH paraM tAta kuravaH kAlacoditAH

07001010c kiM nu svit kuravo 'kArSur nimagnAH zokasAgare
07001011a tad udIrNaM mahat sainyaM trailokyasyApi saMjaya
07001011c bhayam utpAdayet tIvraM pANDavAnAM mahAtmanAm

07001012c yad akArSur nRpatayas tan mamAcakSva saMjaya
07001013  saMjaya uvAca
07001013a zRNu rAjann ekamanA vacanaM bruvato mama
07001013c yat te putrAs tadAkArSur hate devavrate mRdhe
07001014a nihate tu tadA bhISme rAjan satyaparAkrame
07001014c tAvakAH pANDaveyAz ca prAdhyAyanta pRthak pRthak
07001015a vismitAz ca prahRSTAz ca kSatradharmaM nizAmya te
07001015c svadharmaM nindamAnAz ca praNipatya mahAtmane
07001016a zayanaM kalpayAm Asur bhISmAyAmitatejase
07001016c sopadhAnaM naravyAghra zaraiH saMnataparvabhiH
07001017a vidhAya rakSAM bhISmAya samAbhASya parasparam

07001019c tAvakAnAm anIkAni pareSAM cApi niryayuH
07001020a vyAvRtte 'hani rAjendra patite jAhnavIsute
07001020c amarSavazam ApannAH kAlopahatacetasaH
07001021a anAdRtya vacaH pathyaM gAGgeyasya mahAtmanaH

07001023c bhRzam udvignamanaso hInA devavratena te
07001024a patite bharatazreSThe babhUva kuruvAhinI

07001025a vipannasasyeva mahI vAk caivAsaMskRtA yathA
07001025c AsurIva yathA senA nigRhIte purA balau
07001026a vidhaveva varArohA zuSkatoyeva nimnagA
07001026c vRkair iva vane ruddhA pRSatI hatayUthapA
07001027a svAdharSA hatasiMheva mahatI girikandarA
07001027c bhAratI bharatazreSTha patite jAhnavIsute
07001028a viSvagvAtahatA rugNA naur ivAsIn mahArNave
07001028c balibhiH pANDavair vIrair labdhalakSair bhRzArditA
07001029a sA tadAsId bhRzaM senA vyAkulAzvarathadvipA
07001029c viSaNNabhUyiSThanarA kRpaNA draSTum Ababhau
07001030a tasyAM trastA nRpatayaH sainikAz ca pRthagvidhAH
07001030c pAtAla iva majjanto hInA devavratena te
07001030e karNaM hi kuravo 'smArSuH sa hi devavratopamaH
07001031a sarvazastrabhRtAM zreSThaM rocamAnam ivAtithim
07001031c bandhum Apadgatasyeva tam evopAgaman manaH
07001032a cukruzuH karNa karNeti tatra bhArata pArthivAH
07001032c rAdheyaM hitam asmAkaM sUtaputraM tanutyajam
07001033a sa hi nAyudhyata tadA dazAhAni mahAyazAH
07001033c sAmAtyabandhuH karNo vai tam Ahvayata mAciram
07001034a bhISmeNa hi mahAbAhuH sarvakSatrasya pazyataH
07001034c ratheSu gaNyamAneSu balavikramazAliSu
07001034e saMkhyAto 'rdharathaH karNo dviguNaH san nararSabhaH
07001035a rathAtirathasaMkhyAyAM yo 'graNIH zUrasaMmataH
07001035c pitRvittAmbudevezAn api yo yoddhum utsahet
07001036a sa tu tenaiva kopena rAjan gAGgeyam uktavAn
07001036c tvayi jIvati kauravya nAhaM yotsye kathaM cana
07001037a tvayA tu pANDaveyeSu nihateSu mahAmRdhe
07001037c duryodhanam anujJApya vanaM yAsyAmi kaurava
07001038a pANDavair vA hate bhISme tvayi svargam upeyuSi
07001038c hantAsmy ekarathenaiva kRtsnAn yAn manyase rathAn
07001039a evam uktvA mahArAja dazAhAni mahAyazAH
07001039c nAyudhyata tataH karNaH putrasya tava saMmate
07001040a bhISmaH samaravikrAntaH pANDaveyasya pArthiva
07001040c jaghAna samare yodhAn asaMkhyeyaparAkramaH
07001041a tasmiMs tu nihate zUre satyasaMdhe mahaujasi
07001041c tvatsutAH karNam asmArSus tartukAmA iva plavam
07001042a tAvakAs tava putrAz ca sahitAH sarvarAjabhiH
07001042c hA karNa iti cAkrandan kAlo 'yam iti cAbruvan

07001043c agaman no manaH karNaM bandhum AtyayikeSv iva

07001045  vaizaMpAyana uvAca
07001045a tathA karNaM yudhi varaM kIrtayantaM punaH punaH

07002001  saMjaya uvAca
07002001a hataM bhISmam Adhirathir viditvA; bhinnAM nAvam ivAtyagAdhe kurUNAm

07002002a zrutvA tu karNaH puruSendram acyutaM; nipAtitaM zAMtanavaM mahAratham
07002002c athopAyAt tUrNam amitrakarzano; dhanurdharANAM pravaras tadA vRSaH
07002003a hate tu bhISme rathasattame parair; nimajjatIM nAvam ivArNave kurUn
07002003c piteva putrAMs tvarito 'bhyayAt tataH; saMtArayiSyaMs tava putrasya senAm
07002004  karNa uvAca
07002004a yasmin dhRtir buddhiparAkramaujo; damaH satyaM vIraguNAz ca sarve
07002004c astrANi divyAny atha saMnatir hrIH; priyA ca vAg anapAyIni bhISme
07002005a brahmadviSaghne satataM kRtajJe; sanAtanaM candramasIva lakSma
07002005c sa cet prazAntaH paravIrahantA; manye hatAn eva hi sarvayodhAn
07002006a neha dhruvaM kiM cana jAtu vidyate; asmi&l loke karmaNo 'nityayogAt
07002006c sUryodaye ko hi vimuktasaMzayo; bhAvaM kurvItAdya mahAvrate hate
07002007a vasuprabhAve vasuvIryasaMbhave; gate vasUn eva vasuMdharAdhipe
07002007c vasUni putrAMz ca vasuMdharAM tathA; kurUMz ca zocadhvam imAM ca vAhinIm
07002008  saMjaya uvAca
07002008a mahAprabhAve varade nipAtite; lokazreSThe zAMtanave mahaujasi
07002008c parAjiteSu bharateSu durmanAH; karNo bhRzaM nyazvasad azru vartayan
07002009a idaM tu rAdheyavaco nizamya te; sutAz ca rAjaMs tava sainikAz ca ha
07002009c parasparaM cukruzur ArtijaM bhRzaM; tadAzru netrair mumucur hi zabdavat

07002011  karNa uvAca
07002011a jagaty anitye satataM pradhAvati; pracintayann asthiram adya lakSaye
07002011c bhavatsu tiSThatsv iha pAtito raNe; giriprakAzaH kurupuMgavaH katham
07002012a nipAtite zAMtanave mahArathe; divAkare bhUtalam Asthite yathA
07002012c na pArthivAH soDhum alaM dhanaMjayaM; giripravoDhAram ivAnilaM drumAH
07002013a hatapradhAnaM tv idam ArtarUpaM; parair hatotsAham anAtham adya vai
07002013c mayA kurUNAM paripAlyam Ahave; balaM yathA tena mahAtmanA tathA
07002014a samAhitaM cAtmani bhAram IdRzaM; jagat tathAnityam idaM ca lakSaye
07002014c nipAtitaM cAhavazauNDam Ahave; kathaM nu kuryAm aham Ahave bhayam
07002015a ahaM tu tAn kuruvRSabhAn ajihmagaiH; praverayan yamasadanaM raNe caran
07002015c yazaH paraM jagati vibhAvya vartitA; parair hato yudhi zayitAtha vA punaH
07002016a yudhiSThiro dhRtimatidharmatattvavAn; vRkodaro gajazatatulyavikramaH
07002016c tathArjunas tridazavarAtmajo yato; na tad balaM sujayam athAmarair api
07002017a yamau raNe yatra yamopamau bale; sasAtyakir yatra ca devakIsutaH
07002017c na tad balaM kApuruSo 'bhyupeyivAn; nivartate mRtyumukhAd ivAsakRt
07002018a tapo 'bhyudIrNaM tapasaiva gamyate; balaM balenApi tathA manasvibhiH
07002018c manaz ca me zatrunivAraNe dhruvaM; svarakSaNe cAcalavad vyavasthitam
07002019a evaM caiSAM budhyamAnaH prabhAvaM; gatvaivAhaM tAJ jayAmy adya sUta
07002019c mitradroho marSaNIyo na me 'yaM; bhagne sainye yaH sahAyaH sa mitram
07002020a kartAsmy etat satpuruSAryakarma; tyaktvA prANAn anuyAsyAmi bhISmam
07002020c sarvAn saMkhye zatrusaMghAn haniSye; hatas tair vA vIralokaM gamiSye
07002021a saMprAkruSTe ruditastrIkumAre; parAbhUte pauruSe dhArtarASTre
07002021c mayA kRtyam iti jAnAmi sUta; tasmAc chatrUn dhArtarASTrasya jeSye

07002022c sarvAn saMkhye zatrusaMghAn nihatya; dAsyAmy ahaM dhArtarASTrAya rAjyam
07002023a nibadhyatAM me kavacaM vicitraM; haimaM zubhraM maNiratnAvabhAsi
07002023c zirastrANaM cArkasamAnabhAsaM; dhanuH zarAMz cApi viSAhikalpAn
07002024a upAsaGgAn SoDaza yojayantu; dhanUMSi divyAni tathAharantu
07002024c asIMz ca zaktIz ca gadAz ca gurvIH; zaGkhaM ca jAmbUnadacitrabhAsam
07002025a etAM raukmIM nAgakakSyAM ca jaitrIM; jaitraM ca me dhvajam indIvarAbham
07002025c zlakSNair vastrair vipramRjyAnayasva; citrAM mAlAM cAtra baddhvA sajAlAm
07002026a azvAn agryAn pANDurAbhraprakAzAn; puSTAn snAtAn mantrapUtAbhir adbhiH
07002026c taptair bhANDaiH kAJcanair abhyupetAJ; zIghrAJ zIghraM sUtaputrAnayasva
07002027a rathaM cAgryaM hemajAlAvanaddhaM; ratnaiz citraM candrasUryaprakAzaiH
07002027c dravyair yuktaM saMprahAropapannair; vAhair yuktaM tUrNam Avartayasva
07002028a citrANi cApAni ca vegavanti; jyAz cottamAH saMhananopapannAH
07002028c tUNAMz ca pUrNAn mahataH zarANAm; Asajya gAtrAvaraNAni caiva
07002029a prAyAtrikaM cAnayatAzu sarvaM; kanyAH pUrNaM vIrakAMsyaM ca haimam
07002029c AnIya mAlAm avabadhya cAGge; pravAdayantv Azu jayAya bherIH
07002030a prayAhi sUtAzu yataH kirITI; vRkodaro dharmasuto yamau ca
07002030c tAn vA haniSyAmi sametya saMkhye; bhISmAya vaiSyAmi hato dviSadbhiH
07002031a yasmin rAjA satyadhRtir yudhiSThiraH; samAsthito bhImasenArjunau ca
07002031c vAsudevaH sAtyakiH sRJjayAz ca; manye balaM tad ajayyaM mahIpaiH
07002032a taM cen mRtyuH sarvaharo 'bhirakSet; sadApramattaH samare kirITinam
07002032c tathApi hantAsmi sametya saMkhye; yAsyAmi vA bhISmapathA yamAya
07002033a na tv evAhaM na gamiSyAmi teSAM; madhye zUrANAM tat tathAhaM bravImi
07002033c mitradruho durbalabhaktayo ye; pApAtmAno na mamaite sahAyAH

07004001  saMjaya uvAca

07004002a samudra iva sindhUnAM jyotiSAm iva bhAskaraH

07004003a parjanya iva bhUtAnAM pratiSThA suhRdAM bhava
